## Navigation change — where settings live (2026-09-03)

House adopted the information architecture full Budget already uses: **a gear in
the header of every tab**, with the settings behind it. Any row below whose steps
read "Settings → …" or "More → …" must be followed against this map, not against
the pre-2026-09-03 More tab.

| Screen | testID | What is on it |
|---|---|---|
| **More tab** (`tab-settings`) | `settings-screen` | MORE TABS (the tabs that did not fit the bar) · CUSTOMIZATION (`more-customize-tabs`, `settings-row-customization`) · INSIGHTS (`settings-row-ai-insights`). Nothing else. |
| **Settings hub** — the header gear on every tab, and Profile's gear. Route: `simplehouse://house-settings` | `house-settings-screen` | HOME (`settings-row-floor-plans`) · MULTI-PROPERTY · PREFERENCES (`-appearance`, `-currency`, `-region`, `-area-units`, measurement units) · AI (`-ai-housekeeper-settings`, `-persona-settings`, `-ai-providers`) · NOTIFICATIONS & CALENDAR (`-notification-settings`, `-calendar-sync`) · DATA SHARING (`-connect-budget`, `-data-sharing`) · MORE SYMPLY APPS (`-symply-apps`) |
| **Profile** (`simplehouse://profile`) | `profile-screen` | HOME & SHARING (`settings-row-household-members`, `-house-invite`, `-device-sync`, `-house-backup`) · quick sign-in (`settings-row-biometric` / `settings-toggle-biometric`) · Sign out · Danger zone · ABOUT (`profile-terms-of-service`, `profile-privacy-policy`) |

**Every row testID is unchanged** — the rows moved, the ids did not, because they
name the row and the flows already speak them. What changed is the subflow that
gets you there: `subflows/go-house-settings.yaml` for the hub,
`subflows/open-profile.yaml` for the account rows, and `subflows/go-settings-tab.yaml`
only for the three groups still on the More tab.

## §0 Test account prerequisites

Several rows need a pre-built household topology. Build it once and reference it by name in repro steps.

| Account | Role | Purpose |
|---------|------|---------|
| **Primary** (`e2e/credentials.local`, shared test account `a.tekhtelev@gmail.com`) | Owner of HH-A | Runs Groups 1, 3, 4, 6, 7 |
| **Second** | Member of HH-A (non-owner) | Role-limit negatives (HH-023), invite accept (HH-011), chat cross-visibility |
| **Throwaway** | No household | Fresh-signup / onboarding funnel (Group 2), invalid-invite negatives |
| **HH-B** (owned by Primary) | Second household | Household switcher (HH-017), scope-leak negatives |

| Requirement | Detail |
|-------------|--------|
| Brand | `symply-house` Debug or staging EAS build (`com.symply.house`, scheme `house://`) |
| Household fixture | HH-A with ≥2 spaces, ≥1 floor plan, ≥1 garden plan, ≥5 tasks (mixed status), ≥2 reports, ≥2 contractors, ≥1 appliance, ≥1 utility account + 2 bills |
| Media | `e2e/fixtures/` PDF + image for report / garden / floor-plan uploads (`scripts/e2e/seed-fixtures.sh`) |
| iPad | Separate run for `e2e/maestro/tasks/task-edit-ipad.yaml` on an iPad sim |
| Autologin | Debug deep-link autologin only. Release Maestro must type creds via `e2e/maestro/subflows/login-email-if-needed.yaml` |
| Launch | `e2e/maestro/subflows/launch-logged-in.yaml` (authed) or `launch-onboarding.yaml` (funnel) |
| Metro | `__DEV__` network ring buffer (`src/api/e2eNetworkLog.ts`) enabled — required by every **Console verify** cell |
| Runner | `scripts/run-e2e-house.sh` · Maestro config `e2e/maestro/house/config.yaml` |

**Secrets:** never paste tokens, invite codes, or full query strings with PII into RESULTS notes.

## Automated Test Coverage — Execution Groups

Groups are independently runnable unless a dependency is stated. Destructive groups must restore fixtures before the next group runs.

| # | Group | Row IDs | Device / accounts | Run order & fixtures |
|---|-------|---------|-------------------|----------------------|
| 0 | **Fixture seed** | §0 only | iPhone 16 sim, staging | **Run first.** `scripts/e2e/seed-fixtures.sh` then verify HH-A contents. No teardown. |
| 1 | Shell smoke & scroll | `HOUSE-SMOKE-*`, `HOUSE-SCROLL-*` | Primary | Non-destructive, read-only. Safe to re-run any time. Depends on Group 0. |
| 2 | Auth & onboarding | `HOUSE-AUTH-*`, `HOUSE-ONB-*` | Throwaway account, reset sim | **Destructive/isolated** — creates accounts + households. Run on a wiped sim; do not run against HH-A. Teardown: delete created households via `HOUSE-HH-005`. |
| 3 | Core tabs | `HOUSE-HOME-*`, `HOUSE-TASK-*`, `HOUSE-TPLAN-*`, `HOUSE-CHAT-*`, `HOUSE-MIRA-*`, `HOUSE-BUDGET-*` | Primary | Creates + deletes tasks/messages inside HH-A. Each MUTATION row deletes what it created. |
| 4 | Documents & property | `HOUSE-RPT-*`, `HOUSE-UTIL-*`, `HOUSE-PROP-*`, `HOUSE-GARB-*`, `HOUSE-APPL-*`, `HOUSE-CHKL-*` | Primary | Uploads consume `e2e/fixtures/`. Re-seed fixtures after the report-delete rows. |
| 5 | Trades | `HOUSE-CONT-*`, `HOUSE-CMSG-*`, `HOUSE-LABOR-*` | Primary | Contractor/quote/appointment CRUD inside HH-A. Delete created contractors at the end. |
| 6 | Spatial & projects | `HOUSE-GARD-*`, `HOUSE-FP-*`, `HOUSE-HPROJ-*`, `HOUSE-SPACE-*`, `HOUSE-MYHOME-*` | Primary | Floor-plan/garden uploads are heavy; run on a warm sim. Restore the seeded floor plan afterwards. |
| 7 | Platform & household | `HOUSE-HH-*`, `HOUSE-SETT-*`, `HOUSE-CAL-*`, `HOUSE-NOTIF-*`, `HOUSE-PROF-*`, `HOUSE-AIHK-*` | Primary **+ Second** (+ HH-B) | Role/invite rows need the Second account signed in on a parallel sim. `HOUSE-PROF-012` (delete account) must use the Throwaway account only. |
| 8 | Negatives & corners | every `*-VALID-*`, `*-ERROR-*`, `*-CANCEL-*`, `*-CORNER-*` row | Primary + airplane-mode toggle | **Run last.** Offline rows require Settings → Airplane Mode between steps. Restore network + re-seed before re-running Groups 1–7. |

**Cross-group rules**

- No group may leave HH-A without at least one space, one task, and one report — Groups 1/3/4 assume that baseline.
- Rows tagged `Needs 2nd account` in Notes are skipped (mark **N/A**, not Fail) when only one sim is available.
- Rows whose **Automation** cell is `gap` have no runnable asset today; they are manual-tester rows until a flow exists.

## SCROLL — Scroll contracts

Every scrollable House surface must reach its bottom sentinel with sticky chrome (headers, FABs, tab bar) not covering the last row.

## RPT — Reports

Report upload is a **three-leg** flow: `POST …/reports/upload-url` → `PUT` to the signed R2 URL → `POST …/reports/{id}/confirm-upload`, then optional `…/process`. There is no plain `POST /households/{id}/reports` and no `PATCH /households/{id}/reports/{id}`.

## UTIL — Utilities

Real route prefix is `/households/{id}/utilities/…` (`accounts`, `bills`, `property-taxes`, `dashboard`, `analytics`, `bc-assessment`, `municipality`). There is **no** `/utility-accounts`, `/utility-bills` or `/utility-readings` route — meter-reading rows are re-scoped to bills below.

## LF — Local-first (House V2)

Covers the surface H11/H3.5 shipped: `src/screens/house-v2/**`, `src/components/house-v2/**`,
`src/features/house/local/**` and the three Settings rows that reach them. **Zero matrix coverage
before 2026-08-15** — the section did not exist, which is why 49 % of the app's `testID`s were never
referenced by any flow and why `HouseBackupScreen` (26 ids) and `HouseRestoreScreen` (23 ids) had
none at all.

**Backup was rebuilt on 2026-08-26** as a port of `BudgetBackupScreen`: one Settings row, one screen,
five destinations (device / Files / Drive / Dropbox / share), a foreground catch-up scheduler with an
overdue local notification, retention modes, an inline archive list that restores on tap, and the
phrase presented from a background task slot rather than gated in-place. `HouseRestoreScreen` and
`settings-row-house-restore` are GONE — the rows below that named them now name their replacements.
The two things House keeps that Budget has no need for are the property picker (Q15: one archive per
home) and the attachment manifest (Q15: blob bytes are not in the file).

**Isolation rule for this section.** Enrolment is a five-step handshake across two devices, and a
single flow that walks all of it cannot say *which* step broke. Every row below is therefore one
observable step with its own precondition, and the two-device chain lives in
`e2e/maestro/house-multi-member/` where the runner sequences it. A row here must be runnable on ONE
device, in isolation, in any order.

**Gate semantics.** `isHouseLocalFirst()` is brand-default-on for `symply-house`, so these rows
assume it is ON. Rows that assert the OFF behaviour say so explicitly — they are not the same test
with a flag flipped, because the OFF path deliberately renders nothing.

## GARD — Garden plans

Real routes: `GET/DELETE …/garden-plans`, `POST …/garden-plans/upload-url`, `POST/DELETE …/garden-plans/boundary-drafts`, `DELETE …/garden-plans/markers/{id}`. There is **no** plain `POST …/garden-plans`, no garden-plan PATCH, and no "garden plan item" or "garden object" route in `src/api/garden-plans.ts` — those rows are re-scoped or marked deferred below.

## AIHK — AI Housekeeper

Real prefix is `/households/{id}/aihousekeeper/…` (one word). The preferences/insights surface uses the separate `/api/ai-housekeeper/…` prefix. Approvals are `approve` / `cancel` — there is no `reject`.

Data: POST /invite-links/validate returned 200 with `valid: false` and an `error` string (not an HTTP error), because `getActiveLinkByToken` checks `revoked_at`, `isExpired(expires_at)` and `use_count >= max_uses` in that order; no membership row is created. | `invite-unavailable-title` | POST `/invite-links/validate` | `POST /invite-links/validate 200` `valid:false` | Maestro | `e2e/maestro/onboarding/join-invalid-code.yaml` | **ERROR** — flow corrected 2026-07-20 to assert `invite-unavailable-title` unguarded (was asserting `AcceptInviteScreen` copy behind an optional/when guard) |

Assert the "You're Already In" card with the checkmark hero.<br>3. Tap **[ Go to Home ]**. | UI: the already-member branch names the household and offers only **[ Go to Home ]** — no Request button, so a duplicate request cannot be raised from the UI. Data: POST /invite-links/validate returned `alreadyMember: true`, which selects the stage directly; if the user instead taps through a stale ready card, POST /invite-links/request answers `status: 'already_member'`, the client calls GET /households to refresh, and no second join-request row is written. | already-member card | POST `/invite-links/validate`, POST `/invite-links/request` | `POST /invite-links/validate 200` `alreadyMember:true` | Maestro | `gap` | **CORNER** — duplicate-submit guard |
| HOUSE-HH-039 | Short code and long token are interchangeable | 1. Open `simplehouse://j/<code>` and record the rendered household, role and inviter.<br>2. Kill the app, open `simplehouse://join/<token>` for the **same** invite link row.<br>3. Compare both screens field by field. | UI: both routes render the identical `JoinHouseholdScreen` under the same `join-household-screen` testID — the only code difference is the param name (`code` vs `token` in `useLocalSearchParams`); neither route file transforms the value, so the household name, photo, role and "Invited by" row must match exactly. Data: both forms POST the value to /invite-links/validate as `token`; the Worker's `getActiveLinkByToken` matches `token_hash = hash(identifier) OR short_code = identifier`, so the two resolve to the same `household_invite_links` row — a divergence means the short code is bound to the wrong row. `parseJoinToken` accepts both via `/(?:join\|j)\/([^/?#]+)/i`. Note the signed-out capture path in `app/_layout.tsx:250` always re-enters via `router.replace('/join/${token}')`, so `app/j/[code].tsx` is only ever mounted by a warm tap from an authenticated session. | `join-household-screen` | POST `/invite-links/validate` | `POST /invite-links/validate 200`, same household id both times | Maestro, Unit | `src/stores/__tests__/inviteStore.test.ts` | **CORNER** — equivalence guard; the unit suite covers only the URL-parsing half, the BE equivalence half is `gap` |

## SETT — Settings & More hub

Settings persist through `/api/settings` (`GET`, `PUT /api/settings/{key}`, `PUT /api/settings/bulk`, `POST /api/settings/reset`, `POST /api/settings/sync`, `DELETE /api/settings/{key}`). There is **no** `/users/me/settings` route.

Data: BE is none — the hub is composed locally from brand config plus the tabCustomizationStore overrides via resolveEffectiveTabs, so no list refetches and no request fires on mount. | More screen | none | n/a (UI-only) | Maestro | `e2e/maestro/my-home/my-home-screen.yaml` | **LOAD** — Automation downgraded 2026-07-18: cited flow never exercises this criterion (bulk section-link) |

## CUST — Customization & personalization

Covers `src/screens/settings/CustomizationScreen.tsx` (hub, `SettingsStack` route `Customization`, reached from `settings-row-customization`), `src/screens/settings/WidgetCustomizationScreen.tsx` (route `WidgetCustomization`), `src/screens/settings/NavigationCustomizationScreen.tsx` (route `NavigationCustomization`) and `src/screens/settings/TabCustomizationScreen.tsx` (expo-router route `app/customize-tabs.tsx`, reached from `more-customize-tabs`). The three Settings-stack screens carry **zero `testID`s**; only `TabCustomizationScreen` is instrumented (`customize-tabs-screen`, `customize-tabs-save`, `tab-customize-add-{route}`, `tab-customize-remove-{route}`). Stores: `widgetLayoutStore` (`widgets.layout`), `navigationCustomizationStore` (`navigation.tabs`), `tabCustomizationStore` (`navigation.customTabs`) — all persist via `settingsSync.queueSync` → `PUT /api/settings/bulk`. **Two of these editors are persisted no-ops — see Flagged #27/#28.**

Covers `src/screens/ai/AIInsightsDashboardScreen.tsx` (`SettingsStack` route `AIInsightsDashboard`, reached from `settings-row-ai-insights`; House-only, gated by `isHouseBrand()`). All calls go through `src/api/ai-housekeeper.ts` against `/api/ai-housekeeper/*`. The screen carries **zero `testID`s** and there are **no backend tests** for `backend/src/routes/ai-housekeeper.ts` (`backend/src/routes/__tests__/aihousekeeper.test.ts` covers the unrelated `/households/:householdId/aihousekeeper` persona router) — see Flagged #34.

Marking read is a **POST**, not a PATCH, and the list route is `/notifications/history`.
